## User (or harness) — 2026-11-25 14:05:26 UTC

Simulation task in /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7 (branch chipalooza2-documentation). IHP sg13cmos5l PDK at /foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l (PDK_ROOT=/foss/pdks PDK=ihp-sg13cmos5l); ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models, KLayout, magic, netgen installed.

RULES: write ONLY inside the new folder verification/c2_gaps/voltage_stress/ (create it). Do NOT git commit, push, or modify any other file. Run at most ONE ngspice process at a time (other jobs share the 4 CPUs). Put bulky raw data in verification/c2_gaps/voltage_stress/_work/ and add a .gitignore for it. Every file you write gets an SPDX header (SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier / SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0) in the file's comment syntax. US spelling. No invented numbers: every value comes from a simulation or a file you cite.

QUESTION. The PDK process specification (/foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l/libs.doc/doc/SG13CMOS5L_os_process_spec.pdf, Rev. 0.2, sections 2.1.4–2.1.6) states for the HV devices: "VGS ≤ 3.3 V (Maximum) @ 27°C" for HV-NMOS and HV-iNMOS with LG ≥ 0.6 µm and HV-PMOS with LG ≥ 0.5 µm (footnote: 1 FIT in 10 years for HCI), and drain-source breakdown BVDSS ≥ 5.2 V (iNMOS) / 5.3 V. Slot 7 runs from 3.3 V nominal; its characterization went to 3.6 V as a supply-tolerance corner, and its switches and 3.3 V logic use HV devices of W/L 1/0.45 µm (see docs/chipalooza2_as_taped_out/HOW_IT_WORKS.md sizing tables, README §2.2 "Voltage limits (PDK)"). Find, by simulation, the largest terminal voltages every MOS device of the taped-out slot 7 and its two custom pads actually sees, and compare them with those PDK statements.

HOW (reuse what exists; read these first): verification/c2_gaps/dc_curves/ (fetch.sh fetches the design repository at commits 91a7da8b (pads) and 9052bd35 (slot tier-1 decks)), verification/c2_gaps/ac_tran/ (slot.py builds the slot-level deck with schematic / magic-PEX / KLayout-extracted pads), verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg/ (mode and power-up transients), verification/c2_gaps/klayout_pad_diodes/fix_diodes.py (the KLayout-converted pad netlists simulate antenna diodes at the wrong size unless fixed: apply this fix). Use the slot-level deck (bandgap, bias distribution, replica bias, glue logic, both pads) so that every device in slot 7 and both pads is present.
1. DC operating points in the default mode at the five tier-1 PVT points and additionally at 3.6 V for all 5 corners × −40 / 27 / 125 °C, pad input 0, 0.5, 1.0 V and at the ends of the input range (e.g. −0.2 V and 3.3 V on the S2D pad), D2S loaded 1 kΩ ∥ 100 pF and unloaded. Also the disabled state and supplies-off-with-pad-driven state (fail-safe).
2. Transients that can overshoot: power-up ramp of vdd_3v3 / pad ring to 3.6 V, enable on/off edges, a full-swing step on the S2D pad, mode switching.
3. For every MOS instance (flattened), compute VGS, VGD, VDS, VGB (and VSB/VDB) from node voltages (write all node voltages; map instance terminals from the flattened netlist). Report per device type and gate length: the maximum |VGS|, |VGD|, |VDS|, and where (condition, node). Flag every device and condition where |VGS| or |VGD| exceeds 3.3 V, separately for devices with L meeting the spec's LG condition and those below it (0.45 µm), and any |VDS| near BVDSS.
4. Plots: histogram or ranked bar chart of the per-device maximum |VGS| and |VDS| at 3.6 V; a table of the top 20.

DELIVER in verification/c2_gaps/voltage_stress/: README.md (question, method, versions, how to reproduce, what it does NOT show — e.g. the spec's limit is a reliability (HCI) limit at 27 °C, not a breakdown limit), results.md (tables and verdicts, honest), run.sh (reproduces everything from a clean checkout), scripts, csv/ (small summary CSVs only), plots/ (PNG). Report back to me in under 400 words: the largest VGS / VDS found at 3.3 V nominal and at 3.6 V, which devices (type, W/L, block), whether any device with L below the spec's LG condition sees more than 3.3 V, and anything you could not do.

```
# KLayout-extracted pads: antenna diodes simulated at the wrong size

**IHP sg13cmos5l simulation**, ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models and the PDK's `diodes.lib`. Slot 7 tier-1 deck of
the designer (design repository commit 9052bd35), pads in the KLayout-extracted view that the designer's tier-1 /
mode scripts use. Follow-up to [../ac_tran/results.md](../ac_tran/results.md) section 1 (the 10 kHz sine through the
KLayout-extracted pads aborts), [../slot_modes_bg](../slot_modes_bg) (ss / sf −40 °C aborts in the pads' antenna
diodes) and [../dc_curves/results.md](../dc_curves/results.md) section 3 (KLayout-extracted pads shift the round-trip
range ends by up to 55 mV). Status: complete (runs of 2026-11-25).

## Question and hypothesis

The KLayout-extracted pad netlists are converted to simulator syntax by the designer's scripts before simulation.
Hypothesis: the antenna / ESD diodes come out of that conversion at the wrong size (the converted call passes `a=` /
`p=`, the PDK subcircuit reads only `l` / `w`), and that is what makes the sine abort and perhaps shifts the range ends.

## Answer

| item | result |
|---|---|
| conversion bug | **real**. Every `dantenna` / `dpantenna` of both pads is simulated at the PDK default size 0.78 × 0.78 µm. ngspice prints no message about it. |
| 10 kHz sine, tt 27 °C, KLayout pads (the open item of ../ac_tran) | **cured by the size fix**. Runs through
… [21712 more characters cut]
```

```
## 2. Electrical function

### 2.1 Signal and current flow

![Slot 7 signal and current flow: harness controls into s7_ctrl; bg_core_pd, bias_dist_pd and pad_ref_rb supply the pads' reference currents and vcm / vref; S2D pad, switch matrix and D2S pad form the signal path; analog_bus0..3 taps](pix/slot7_signal_flow.svg)

*Figure: signal and current flow of slot 7 as taped out (`158afc5`). Connections from the netlist as described in [glue_logic.md](glue_logic.md) (switch matrix §4, same-net joins and `bias_dist_pd` configuration §5). Generated by [`pix/make_signal_flow.py`](pix/make_signal_flow.py) (SVG; [PNG](pix/slot7_signal_flow.png)).*

The same as plain text: [pix/slot7_signal_flow.txt](pix/slot7_signal_flow.txt).

Default function (enable = 1): the pad signal at `s7_an[0]` is reproduced at `s7_an[1]`, shifted from the S2D `vref` (~0.49 V) to the D2S `vref`
(= `vcm`, ~1.26 V): D2S out = `vcm` + (S2D in − `vref`), for an input of 0 … 1 V. Swap crosses the differential pair (inverts about `vcm`).
The point of the slot is to show the two pad buffers working together with on-slot references and fail-safe enables.

### 2.2 Supplies, enable and fail-safe

- **Supplies:** `vdd_3v3` / `vss_3v3` for everything; `vdd_1v2` only gates the 12 pull-downs of `dig_out[11:0]` (`s7_doutpd`, NMOS 0.5 µm / 0.45 µm hv, held low).
  The slot works with only `vdd_3v3` switched on: the enables do not need a 1.2 V presence.
- **Enable:** harness `enable` = 1 turns the slot on in the default mode
… [11529 more characters cut]
```

```
* SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
* slot_7: the slot-7 circuit (tier 1) with with the custom pads (proposal, 2026-11-08; docs/slot7_hookup/CONCEPT.md).
*   Harness enable -> ls_up_fs (en_ctrl_3v3 v2's shifter) -> en_3v3 / en_3v3_b for everything; s7_ctrl (above): loop
*   S2D -> D2S direct or crossed, S2D outputs to bus0/1, bus0/1 to the D2S inputs; bandgap core -> bias_dist_pd input 0 -> iout0 pad_ref_rb, iout1 S2D iref, iout2 D2S iref (iout3 spare,
*   off); pad_ref_rb (on the slot's vdd_3v3: the pad ring is not reachable inside the slot) -> vcm to S2D vcm and D2S
*   vref, vref to S2D vref; analog_bus2 / 3 = vcm / vref, isolated by the harness's own per-slot bus switches (reg 0x51 bits 6 / 7).
* Ports: the slot's harness ports used here, then the 13 custom-pad nets (names as in the harness's custom-io route).
* dig_out0..11 are held low by s7_doutpd (needs the slot's vdd_1v2 on); unused, not listed: dig_in6..23, clk, reset,
* ibias0/1, vbias.
* s7_ctrl: mode control and switch matrix of slot 7 (tier 1, 2026-11-08, Christoph's spec):
*   dig_in0 = swap (loop crossed), dig_in1 = mon (S2D outputs -> analog_bus0/1, loop stays on),
*   dig_in2 = feed (analog_bus0/1 -> D2S inputs, loop off), dig_in5 = ext (pad_ref_rb's vcm / vref disconnected
*   from the pads, which then take vcm / vref from analog_bus2 / 3), active only with the key dig_in3 = 1, dig_in4 = 0;
*   any other key (e.g. all dig_in equal after a harness reset) = def
```

```
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Starvation check: ring (iovdd) current of the two custom pads and of pad_ref_rb with the slot off, i.e. no iref,
enables anywhere (pad_ref_rb has no pad pin: its line shows pad_V 0).

usage (repo env, from this directory): python3 -I run_starve.py   -> results/starve.txt (decks and logs in runs/)

The pads are not changed: their extracted layout netlists (netlist/layout/*_klayout.cir of the two pad macros) are
read and converted to simulator syntax in runs/. iovdd 3.6 V; iref, vcm / vref / vinp / vinn and the core outputs
float (1 Gohm to iovss); en_3v3 and en_3v3_b each swept 0 ... 3.6 V in 0.3 V steps (169 combinations, as a switched-off
slot can leave them anywhere); the pad pin driven at 0, 1.8 and 3.6 V. Reported: the largest and smallest iovdd
current over the 169 combinations.
"""
import re
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MAC = HERE.parents[2]
RUNS = HERE / "runs"
RES = HERE / "results"
PADS = {"s2d": ("sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff", "X1 en enb iovdd 0 iref outn outp padn vcm vref",
                ["iref", "vcm", "vref", "outp", "outn"]),
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Terminal voltages of every MOS device of slot 7 and its two custom pads (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, ngspice).

Usage: python3 -I vs.py <work_dir> <job> [<job> ...]
  job = <kind>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>[:<variant>]        e.g. dc:tt:27:3.3:open, pu:ff:-40:3.6, topo:tt:27:3.3
  kind   topo  flattened netlist (ngspice 'listing e'): every MOS instance with its four terminal nodes -> topo.csv
         dc    default mode, enable 1.2 V, DC sweep of the S2D pad source -0.2 ... max(3.3, vdd) V in 50 mV steps;
               variant open (D2S pad 1 Gohm only) | 1k (1 kOhm from the D2S pad to vterm = v(vref_d2s), as
               ../ac_tran; 100 pF is irrelevant at DC) | 1kgnd (1 kOhm from the D2S pad to ground)
         dcoff disabled: enable 0, otherwise as dc open
         fs    fail-safe, slot vdd_3v3 = 0 and vdd_1v2 = 0, enable 0, pad ring iovdd = <vdd>; both pads driven from
               one source swept 0 ... <vdd>, each through 1 kOhm
         fs0   fail-safe, all supplies 0 V (vdd_3v3 = iovdd = vdd_1v2 = 0, enable 0), both pads driven as fs
         pu    power-up transient: vdd_3v3, iovdd (and vdd_1v2 to 1.2 V) ramp 0 -> V in 10 us (from all nodes at 0 V,
               uic), enable 0 -> 1.2 V at 20 us (100 ns edge), S2D pad 0.5 V, end 40 us
               variant fast: ramp in 1 us, enable already 1.2 V from t = 0, end 20 us
         en    enable edges at V: from the enabled operating point, enable 1.2 -> 0 V at 2 us, 0 -> 1.2 V at 22 us
               (10 ns edges), end 42 us; S2D pad 0.5 V
         step  full-swing S2D pad steps from the enabled operating point (pad 0 V): 0 -> V at 2 us, V -> -0.2 V at
               12 us, -0.2 -> 0.5 V at 22 us (10 ns edges), end 32 us; variant open | 100p (1 kOhm to vterm + 100 pF)
         mode  mode switching at V (mode bits through the key, 10 ns edges): default, swap at 2 us, mon 12 us,
               feed 22 us, ext 32 us, all_high 42 us, default 52 us, end 62 us; analog_bus0/1 driven (10 kOhm to 1.45 /
               1.05 V) during feed, analog_bus2/3 (10 kOhm to 1.30 / 0.50 V) during ext (voltage-controlled switches,
               as ../slot_modes_bg); S2D pad 0.5 V
Writes <work_dir>/dev/<tag>.csv.gz (per MOS instance: max |VGS|, |VGD|, |VDS|, |VGB|, |VSB|, |VDB| and where),
<work_dir>/nodes/<tag>.csv.gz (max / min of every node), <work_dir>/log/<tag>.log; the raw file is deleted after
evaluation unless KEEP_RAW=1.

Deck: the designer's tier-1 slot deck (design repository commit 9052bd35, docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_tier1.py,
imported) as ../ac_tran/slot.py builds it for the KLayout view: slot_7 source netlist (s7_ctrl glue logic, s7_doutpd,
ls_up_fs, hv_logic), bg_core_pd schematic, pad_ref_rb, bias_dist_pd and both custom pads KLayout-extracted (converted
by run_tier1.wrapped()), the pads' antenna diodes given their extracted size by ../klayout_pad_diodes/fix_diodes.py.
vdd_3v3 = iovdd = <vdd>; pads' vdd / vss pins on iovdd / 0 (as run_tier1.py; the extracted pads have no separate
core-supply pins). .option gmin=1e-12 as the designer. If a DC sweep fails, it is rerun once with rshunt=1e12 (1 Tohm
from every node to ground, solver aid as ../ac_tran); transients use method=gear cshunt=1e-15 (as run_tier1.py's own
transient) and a 10 ns maximum time step (as ../slot_modes_bg); a failed transient is rerun once with a 2 ns maximum
step. Aids are recorded in the log and in the dev CSV header.
"""
```

```
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Minimal reader of an ngspice binary raw file (one real plot): read(path) -> (names, scale, data[points, vars])."""
import numpy as np


def read(path):
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        buf = f.read()
    k = buf.index(b'Binary:\n')
    head = buf[:k].decode('latin-1').splitlines()
    nv = npt = None
    names = []
    invars = False
    for ln in head:
        if ln.startswith('No. Variables:'):
            nv = int(ln.split(':')[1])
        elif ln.startswith('No. Points:'):
            npt = int(ln.split(':')[1])
        elif ln.startswith('Flags:') and 'complex' in ln:
            raise ValueError('complex raw file not supported')
        elif ln.startswith('Variables:'):
            invars = True
        elif invars and ln.strip():
            names.append(ln.split()[1])
    assert nv == len(names), (nv, len(names))
    data = np.frombuffer(buf, dtype='<f8', offset=k + 8)
    npt = min(npt, data.size // nv)
    data = data[:npt * nv].reshape(npt, nv)
    return names, data[:, 0], data
```

```bash
p='vs.py'
s=open(p).read()
old="""        pr = subprocess.run(['ngspice', '-b', f.name], cwd=rdir, capture_output=True, text=True)
        out = pr.stdout + '\\n--- stderr ---\\n' + pr.stderr"""
new="""        tmo = TMO_TRAN if kind in ('pu', 'en', 'step', 'mode') else TMO_DC
        try:
            pr = subprocess.run(['ngspice', '-b', f.name], cwd=rdir, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=tmo)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            so = e.stdout.decode(errors='replace') if isinstance(e.stdout, bytes) else (e.stdout or '')
            pr = subprocess.CompletedProcess(e.cmd, -9, so, f'TIMEOUT after {tmo} s (killed)')
        out = pr.stdout + '\\n--- stderr ---\\n' + pr.stderr"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace("MODESEQ = [", "TMO_DC = int(os.environ.get('VS_TMO_DC', '240'))      # s per ngspice run (a stalled DC sweep is killed)\nTMO_TRAN = int(os.environ.get('VS_TMO_TRAN', '1800'))\nMODESEQ = [",1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
grep -n "TMO" vs.py | head
```

```
Limits compared (PDK process specification SG13CMOS5L Rev. 0.2, sections 2.1.4 ... 2.1.6, libs.doc/doc/
SG13CMOS5L_os_process_spec.pdf): VGS <= 3.3 V (maximum) at 27 C for HV-NMOS with LG >= 0.6 um and HV-PMOS with
LG >= 0.5 um (footnote: 1 FIT in 10 years for HCI); breakdown BVDSSNHV013 min 5.3 V, BVDSSPHV04 max -5.3 V
(i.e. |BVDSS| >= 5.3 V), BVDSSNIHV045 min 5.2 V (HV-iNMOS; no iNMOS is used in slot 7).
"""
import csv
import gzip
import re
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402

WORK, OUT = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve(), Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
VLIM = 3.3
LG = {'sg13_hv_nmos': 0.6, 'sg13_hv_pmos': 0.5}
BVDSS = {'sg13_hv_nmos': 5.3, 'sg13_hv_pmos': 5.3}
QTY = ('vgs', 'vgd', 'vds', 'vgb', 'vsb', 'vdb')
KIND_TXT = {'dc': 'DC, enabled default mode, S2D pad swept', 'fs': 'fail-safe: slot supplies 0, ring on, pads driven',
            'fs0': 'fail-safe: all supplies 0, pads driven', 'off': 'disabled (enable 0), pad ramped',
            'pu': 'power-up transient', 'en': 'enable off / on edges', 'step': 'full-swing S2D pad steps',
            'mode': 'mode switching', 'topo': 'topology run (DC tt 27 C 3.3 V, as dc open)'}


def block(inst):
    p = inst.split('.')
    if p[1] == 'x2s':
        return 'S2D pad'
    if p[1] == 'x2d':
        return 'D2S pad'
    b = p[2]
    if b == 'xc':
        sub = p[3]
        if sub.startswith('xl'):
            return 's7_ctrl ls_up_fs'
        if sub.startswith('xt'):
            return 's7_ctrl tg_hv switches'
        return 's7_ctrl logic'
    return {'xbg': 'bg_core_pd', 'xd': 'bias_dist_pd', 'xr': 'pad_ref_rb', 'xdo': 's7_doutpd'}.get(b, b)


def load_topo():
    rows = []
    with open(WORK / 'topo.csv') as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            r['L'] = round(float(r['l']) * 1e6, 4)
            r['W'] = round(float(r['w']) * 1e6, 4)
            r['NF'] = int(round(float(r['nf'] or 1)))
            r['block'] = block(r['inst'])
            r['type'] = 'HV-NMOS' if 'nmos' in r['dev'] else 'HV-PMOS'
            r['spec_L'] = r['L'] >= LG[r['dev']] - 1e-9
            r['cap'] = r['d'] == r['s']        # drain = source: MOS capacitor (or a shorted dummy)
            r['name'] = '.'.join(r['inst'].split('.')[:-1])  # subckt instance name (without the PDK's inner N)
            rows.append(r)
    return rows


def parse_tag(tag):
    m = re.match(r'^(\w+?)_(\w\w)_(-?\d+)_([\d.]+)(?:_(\w+))?$', tag)
    kind, c, t, v, var = m.groups()
    return dict(kind=kind, mos=c, temp=int(t), vdd=float(v), var=var or '')


def load_runs():
    runs = {}
    for f in sorted((WORK / 'dev').glob('*.csv.gz')):
        tag = f.name[:-7]
        info = parse_tag(tag)
        with gzip.open(f, 'rt') as fh:
            head = fh.readline()
            m = re.search(r'aid (\S+), (\d+) points', head)
            info['aid'], info['npts'] = (m.group(1).rstrip(','), int(m.group(2))) if m else ('?', 0)
            rd = csv.DictReader(fh)
            info['dev'] = {r['inst']: r for r in rd}
        runs[tag] = info
    return runs


def failed_runs(runs):
    out = []
    for f in sorted((WORK / 'log').glob('*.log')):
        tag = f.name[:-4]
        if tag not in runs:
            txt = f.read_text(errors='replace')
            m = re.findall(r'(doAnalyses:[^\n]*|TIMEOUT[^\n]*|Error: Transient op failed[^\n]*|timestep too small[^\n]*)',
                           txt)
            t = re.findall(r'tran simulation\(s\) aborted|time =

```

```bash
    if not topo_csv().exists():
        return ''
    nodes = set()
    for r in load_topo():
        nodes |= {r['d'], r['g'], r['s'], r['b']}
    nodes = sorted(nodes - {'0'}) + ['padin', 'padout', 'iovdd', 'vdd_3v3', 'enable', 'vterm', 'v12']
    nodes = list(dict.fromkeys(nodes))
    return ''.join('save ' + ' '.join(f'v({n})' for n in nodes[i:i + 20]) + '\\n' for i in range(0, len(nodes), 20))


def parse_listing(text):'''
```

## Assistant — 2026-11-25 14:35:37 UTC

**Tool call: `Write`**

file: `/home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/voltage_stress/run.sh`

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Terminal voltages of every MOS device of slot 7 and its two custom pads (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, ngspice-47 with
# the IHP OSDI models): reproduces csv/, plots/ and tables.md of this folder from a checkout of this repository plus
# the design-repository commits 91a7da8b / 9052bd35 (fetched by ../dc_curves/fetch.sh, shallow and blobless).
# Usage: run.sh [<work_dir>] [fetch|sim|analyze|all]   (default ./_work, all)
#   sim runs the jobs of jobs.txt one after the other (one ngspice process at a time); a job whose result exists in
#   <work_dir>/dev is skipped (FORCE=1 reruns everything). Raw files are deleted after evaluation (KEEP_RAW=1 keeps them).
# Needs PDK_ROOT with ihp-sg13cmos5l, ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models, python3 with numpy / matplotlib,
# git with network access to github.com.
set -euo pipefail
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "${1:-$HERE/_work}")
STEP=${2:-all}
export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks} PDK=ihp-sg13cmos5l
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1
mkdir -p "$WORK"

fetch() { "$HERE/../dc_curves/fetch.sh" "$WORK"; }

tagof() {  # job -> run tag (as vs.py)
  local IFS=:; set -- $1
  echo "$1_$2_$3_$4${5:+_$5}"
}

sim() {
  local j
  grep -v '^#' "$HERE/jobs.txt" | while read -r j; do
    [ -n "$j" ] || continue
    if [ "${FORCE:-0}" != 1 ] && [ -f "$WORK/dev/$(tagof "$j").csv.gz" ]; then continue; fi
    python3 -I "$HERE/vs.py" "$WORK" "$j"
  done
}

analyze() {
  python3 -I "$HERE/analyze.py" "$WORK" "$HERE"
}

case "$STEP" in
  all) fetch; sim; analyze ;;
  fetch|sim|analyze) $STEP ;;
  *) echo "unknown step $STEP"; exit 1 ;;
esac
```

```
## 6. `bias_dist_pd` and the fail-safe enables

### 6.1 `bias_dist_pd`: switchable 1 : 1 distribution

Sources: `macros/bias_dist_pd/README.md`, `netlist/source/bias_dist_pd.spice` (S); configuration in the slot:
[glue_logic.md](glue_logic.md) §5. Chain: input TGs → NMOS diode NI 6 / 6 → NM 6 / 6 → PMOS diode PD 5 / 6 → outputs
PO0 … PO3 5 / 6, each behind a TG; enabled inputs add in NI. In the slot input 0 is the bandgap; outputs 0 / 1 / 2 go to
`pad_ref_rb`, S2D, D2S (output 3 off). "Devices: those of `bias_tree_pd` (NI 6u/6u like the tree's NI, PD / PO 5u/6u
like BP)". The two pads get the inner pair PO1 / PO2 next to PD, "mirror images of each other: both pads get the matched
pair" (designer's decision of 2026-11-08). Each leg is a `bdp_slice`: `nand2_hv(en_3v3, en_k)` → `inv_hv` → `tg_hv`, all
1 / 0.45 µm hv. Disabled: all TGs open, TDN pulls `ni` to vss, SVPD pulls `vpd` to vdd.

Simulated ([replica_bias](../../verification/c2_gaps/replica_bias/results.md) §3): systematic copy ratio +0.49 … +1.56 %,
falling by about 0.5 % from 0.5 to 2.5 V output voltage ("output conductance of PO / the switch"); mismatch σ 1.0 …
1.2 % per output; the pads' pair `iout1 − iout2` σ 0.36 … 0.41 % because it shares NI / NM / PD; off leakage ≤ 145 pA.
```

```
### 6.2 Fail-safe enables: `ls_up_fs`

Sources: `macros/en_ctrl_3v3/README.md`, `netlist/source/en_ctrl_3v3.spice` (S); slot use: [glue_logic.md](glue_logic.md) §2.

![ls_up_fs schematic](pix/schematics/ls_up_fs.svg)

*Figure 6.2: `ls_up_fs` (S), the level shifter of every slot input bit (7 instances in `s7_ctrl`).*

Problem it solves (README "Why v2"): v1 sensed the slot supplies with ratioed detectors and shifted with a
latch that needs the slot's 1.2 V; with that 1.2 V off and `enable` high its latch drew up to 152 µA. v2 uses the fact
that the harness drives `enable` and `dig_in` from an always-on 1.2 V rail, so the bits are valid even while the slot's
`vdd_1v2` is off, and shifts each bit **without any 1.2 V supply**, on the slot's switched `vdd_3v3` only:

- **XN1** (hv NMOS 1 / 0.45, gate = the 1.2 V input) pulls node `x` low when the input is 1.
- **Leaker:** 10 hv PMOS 0.3 / 20 in series (effectively 0.3 / 200), gates on the same input. With the input at 0 it has
  full gate drive and pulls `x` high; with the input at 1.2 V it is "2–5× weaker" and loses to XN1. It is the only
  static current, and only while the input is high: 28 … 92 nA over corners (netlist header).
- **Latch XPF** (hv PMOS 0.3 / 5, gate `y` = NOT `x`): once `x` is high it holds it (hysteresis) against XN1's leakage.
- Three `inv_hv` (1 / 0.45) give `y`, `out`, `out_b`.

The input must be a real harness level: XN1 conducts 0.6 … 8 µA at a 0.67 V gate. Monte Carlo was not run; the README's
argument 
… [875 more characters cut]
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Solver check of the transient overshoots: the same enable-edge transient with different time-step limits /
integration methods, node waveforms around the edges, and the largest |VGS| / |VDS| per setting.

Usage: python3 -I edges.py <work_dir> <out_dir> <base_job> <aid> [<aid> ...]
  base_job e.g. en:tt:27:3.6 ; aid '' (the setting of the stress runs: gear, cshunt 1 fF, max step 10 ns) or one of
  step2n / step05n / step01n / trap / nocshunt (vs.py variant chk_<aid>). The runs are made with vs.py (KEEP_RAW=1).
Writes <out_dir>/csv/solver_check_<base>.csv and <out_dir>/plots/solver_check_<base>.png.
Nodes plotted: the bg_core_pd start-up chain node net1 (largest excursion found), the bias_dist_pd spare output isp,
vdd_3v3 and the slot's en_b.
"""
import csv
import gzip
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import rawread  # noqa: E402

WORK, OUT = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve(), Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
BASE, AIDS = sys.argv[3], sys.argv[4:]
NODES = ['xs.xbg.net1', 'xs.xbg.net7', 'xs.isp', 'xs.en_b']
WIN = {'en': [(1.95, 2.45), (21.95, 22.45)], 'off': [(0.95, 1.45)]}
LAB = {'': 'gear, cshunt 1 fF, max step 10 ns (stress runs)', 'step2n': 'max step 2 ns', 'step05n': 'max step 0.5 ns',
       'step01n': 'max step 0.1 ns', 'trap': 'trapezoidal', 'nocshunt': 'gear, no cshunt'}


def tag_of(job):
    p = job.split(':')
    return f'{p[0]}_{p[1]}_{p[2]}_{p[3]}' + (f'_{p[4]}' if len(p) > 4 and p[4] else '')


def main():
    kind = BASE.split(':')[0]
    res, waves = [], {}
    env = dict(os.environ, KEEP_RAW='1')
    for aid in AIDS:
        job = BASE + (f':chk_{aid}' if aid else '')
        tag = tag_of(job)
        raw = WORK / 'runs' / tag / f'{tag}.raw'
        if not raw.exists():
            subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-I', str(HERE / 'vs.py'), str(WORK), job], env=env, check=False)
        if not raw.exists():
            res.append([aid or 'default', LAB[aid], 'FAILED', '', '', '', '', ''])
            continue
        names, x, data = rawread.read(raw)
        idx = {n.lower(): i for i, n in enumerate(names)}
        w = {n: data[:, idx[f'v({n})']] for n in NODES}
        waves[aid] = (x, w)
        with gzip.open(WORK / 'dev' / f'{tag}.csv.gz', 'rt') as fh:
            fh.readline()
            rows = list(csv.DictReader(fh))
        mg = max(rows, key=lambda r: abs(float(r['vgs'])))
        md = max(rows, key=lambda r: abs(float(r['vds'])))
        res.append([aid or 'default', LAB[aid], len(x), f"{w['xs.xbg.net1'].max():.3f}", f"{w['xs.xbg.net1'].min():.3f}",
                    f"{w['xs.isp'].max():.3f}", f"{abs(float(mg['vgs'])):.3f} {mg['inst']}",
                    f"{abs(float(md['vds'])):.3f} {md['inst']}"])
    base = BASE.replace(':', '_')
    (OUT / 'csv').mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / 'plots').mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(OUT / 'csv' / f'solver_check_{base}.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:
        wr = csv.writer(f)
        wr.writerow(['setting', 'what', 'time_points', 'net1_max_V', 'net1_min_V', 'isp_max_V', 'max_abs_vgs_V_device',
                     'max_abs_vds_V_device'])
        wr.writerows(res)
    wins = WIN.get(kind, [(0, float(max(x[-1] for x, _ in waves.values())) * 1e6)])
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(len(NODES), len(wins), figsize=(5 * len(wins), 2.0 * len(NODES)), squeeze=False,
                            sharex='col')
    for j, (a, b) in enumerate(wins):
        for i, n in enumerate(NODES):
            ax = axs[i][j]
            for aid, (x, w) in waves.items():
                m = (x >= a * 1e-6) & (x <= b * 1e-6)
                ax.plot(x[m] * 1e6, w[n][m], lw=1, label=LAB[aid])
            ax.set_ylabel(f'v({n}) [V]', fontsize=8)
            ax.g
… [352 more characters cut]
```

```
def main():
    topo = load_topo()
    runs = load_runs()
    fails = failed_runs(runs)
    (OUT / 'csv').mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / 'plots').mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    nodeinfo = {r['inst']: r for r in topo}

    # per device x supply class x temperature filter: maximum of each |quantity| and where
    def devmax(sel):
        res = {}
        for r in topo:
            best = {q: (0.0, None, None) for q in QTY}
            for tag, info in runs.items():
                if not sel(info):
                    continue
                d = info['dev'][r['inst']]
                for q in QTY:
                    v = float(d[q])
                    if abs(v) > abs(best[q][0]):
                        best[q] = (v, tag, d['x_' + q])
            res[r['inst']] = best
        return res

    classes = {
        '3.3': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.3,
        '3.6': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.6,
        '3.3_27C': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.3 and i['temp'] == 27,
        '3.6_27C': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.6 and i['temp'] == 27,
        '3.0': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.0,
        '3.3_enabled': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.3 and i['kind'] in ('dc', 'topo', 'step', 'mode', 'en', 'pu'),
        '3.6_enabled': lambda i: i['vdd'] == 3.6 and i['kind'] in ('dc', 'topo', 'step', 'mode', 'en', 'pu'),
    }
    M = {k: devmax(f) for k, f in classes.items()}

    # --- per-device CSV (all devices, 3.3 V and 3.6 V classes)
```

```bash
              '3.6_tran'):
        for spec in (True, False):

            overh = [r for r in sel if gx(k, r, True) > VLIM]
            ov_gs = [r for r in sel if abs(M[k][r['inst']]['vgs'][0]) > VLIM]
            mx = max((gx(k, r) for r in sel), default=0)
            mh = max((gx(k, r, True) for r in sel), default=0)
            blocks = defaultdict(int)

                blocks[r['block']] += 1
            nruns = sum(1 for i in runs.values() if classes[k](i))

                         len(ov_gs), len(over), len(overh), f'{mx:.3f}', f'{mh:.3f}',
                         ', '.join(f'{b} {n}' for b, n in sorted(blocks.items()))])
            for r in over:

                v2, t2, x2 = M[k][r['inst']]['vgd']

                           '|VGS| or |VGD| > 3.3 V', 'same, held >= 100 ns', 'max of |VGS|, |VGD| [V]',
                           'same, held >= 100 ns [V]', 'by block (|VGS| or |VGD| > 3.3 V)'], rows))
    with open(OUT / 'csv' / 'flags_over_3v3.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(['runs', 'inst', 'block', 'type', 'W_um', 'L_um', 'meets_spec_LG', 'vgs_V', 'run_vgs', 'x_vgs',
                    'vgd_V', 'run_vgd', 'x_vgd', 'vgx_held_V'])
        w.writerows(flagrows)

    # --- devices meeting the spec LG with |VGS| or |VGD| > 3.3 V: list them

                             f'{abs(v1):.3f}', cond_txt(t1, x1, 'vgs'), f'{abs(v2):.3f}', cond_txt(t2, x2, 'vgd'),
                             f'{gx(k, r, True):.3f}', f"{abs(M[k][r['inst']]['vds'][0]):.3f}",
                             f"g {r['g']}, s {r['s']}, d {r['d']}"])
            lines.append(md_table(['device', 'block', 'type', 'W/L [um]', 'd = s (capacitor)', '|VGS| [V]',
                                   'where', '|VGD| [V]', 'where', 'max of |VGS|, |VGD| held >= 100 ns [V]',
                                   'max |VDS| [V]', 'nodes'], rows))

    # --- BVDSS
```

```bash
        ax.plot(range(len(srt)), yh, '_', ms=3, color='#6a3d9a', label='same device, 3.6 V, held >= 100 ns')
        ax.plot(range(len(srt)), y3, '.', ms=2, color='#222222', label='same device, max over the 3.3 V runs')"""
assert a in s; s=s.replace(a,b)
```

```
<!--
SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
-->
# Terminal voltages of every MOS device in slot 7 and its two custom pads, compared with the PDK's HV limits

**IHP sg13cmos5l simulation**, ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models (PSP 103). The slot-level deck (bandgap, bias
distribution, replica bias, glue logic, both custom pads) of the designer's tier-1 testbench, with every MOS instance
of slot 7 and of both pads present: 499 MOS devices, all HV (`sg13_hv_nmos` 200, `sg13_hv_pmos` 299; no LV device and
no iNMOS). Numbers, tables and verdicts: **[results.md](results.md)**; all generated tables: [tables.md](tables.md).

## Question

The PDK process specification (`/foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l/libs.doc/doc/SG13CMOS5L_os_process_spec.pdf`, Rev. 0.2,
md5 `b0275fe63f332765fe1527a2d050c0fe`) states:

| section | device | statement (quoted) | breakdown (table row, min / max, measured at W x L) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2.1.4 | HV-NMOS | "VGS ≤ 3.3 V (Maximum) @ 27°C for LG ≥ 0.6 µm" | BVDSSNHV013: min 5.3 V, target 6.1 V (10 x 0.45 µm²) |
| 2.1.5 | HV-PMOS | "VGS ≤ 3.3 V (Maximum) @ 27°C for LG ≥ 0.5 µm" | BVDSSPHV04: target −6.3 V, max −5.3 V (10 x 0.4 µm²) |
| 2.1.6 | HV-iNMOS | "VGS ≤ 3.3 V (Maximum) @ 27°C for LG ≥ 0.6 µm" | BVDSSNIHV045: min 5.2 V, target 6.1 V (10 x 0.45 µm²) |

each with the footnote "1 FIT in 10 years for HCI: for details see SG13G2 Qualification Report". Slot 7 runs from
3.3 V nominal; its characterization went to 3.6 V as a supply-tolerance corner; its switches and 3.3 V logic are HV
devices of W/L 1/0.45 µm ([HOW_IT_WORKS.md](../../../docs/chipalooza2_as_taped_out/HOW_IT_WORKS.md) sizing tables,
[README §2.2](../../../docs/chipalooza2_as_taped_out/README.md#22-supplies-enable-and-fail-safe) "Voltage limits
(PDK)"). Which terminal voltages does every MOS device actually see, and how do they compare with these statements?

## Method

### Deck (`vs.py`)

The designer's tier-1 slot deck (design repository `tatzelbrumm/sg13cmos5l_cm_ip__single2diff2single`, commit
`9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9`, `docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_tier1.py`, imported unchanged), built as
[../ac_tran/slot.py](../ac_tran/slot.py) builds it for the KLayout pad view:

- `slot_7` source netlist (`macros/slot_7/netlist/source/slot_7.spice`: `s7_ctrl` glue logic with 7 `ls_up_fs`,
  13 `nand2_hv`, 12 `inv_hv`, 10 `tg_hv`; `s7_doutpd`), `hv_logic` and `en_ctrl_3v3` source netlists;
- `bg_core_pd` schematic netlist (`netlist/schematic/bg_core_pd_magic.spice`), as in the designer's runs;
- `pad_ref_rb`, `bias_dist_pd` and both custom pads (`sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff` = S2D, `sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single`
  = D2S) **KLayout-extracted** from the layout (`netlist/layout/*_klayout.cir`, converted and port-wrapped by
  `run_tier1.wrapped()`), with the antenna-diode fix of [../klayout_pad_diodes/fix_diodes.py](../klayout_pad_diodes/fix_diodes.py)
  applied (imported, unchanged) and the KLayout instance names `X$<n>` written `X_<n>` (a `$` would be expanded as a
  variable in ngspice's `.control` section). The extracted views merge parallel fingers into one device of the summed
  width, so the device count of the pads is that of the extracted netlist (S2D 65, D2S 64 MOS).
- `vdd_3v3` = `iovdd` = the supply under test; the pads' `vdd` / `vss` pins on `iovdd` / ground as in `run_tier1.py`
  (the extracted pads have no separate core-supply pins); `vdd_1v2` 1.2 V (only the `s7_doutpd` gates); harness
  `enable` 1.2 V; mode bits through the key as `../slot_modes_bg/slot_modes.py`; analog buses 1 GΩ to ground unless
  driven; D2S pad 1 GΩ to ground plus the load of the job; MOS corner on hv and lv, `res_typ`, `cap_typ`, pnp `typ`.
- `.option gmin=1e-12` (as the designer). Solver aids, recorded per run in `csv/runs.csv`: DC sweeps that fail are
  rerun with `rshunt=1e12`; the fail-safe DC sweeps (unpowered nodes held by leakage only) always run with it.
  Transients use `method=gear cshunt=1e-15` (as `run_tier1.p
… [9484 more characters cut]
```

```bash
cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/voltage_stress && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='analyze.py'
s=open(p).read()
a="""    # --- devices meeting the spec LG with |VGS| or |VGD| > 3.3 V: list them"""
b="""    # --- distribution of the per-device gate stress over bins
    edges = [0, 3.3, 3.35, 3.5, 3.6, 3.65, 3.8, 4.0, 5.0, 99]
    binlab = ['<= 3.3', '3.3 ... 3.35', '3.35 ... 3.5', '3.5 ... 3.6', '3.6 ... 3.65', '3.65 ... 3.8', '3.8 ... 4.0',
              '4.0 ... 5.0', '> 5.0']
    lines.append('\\n## Number of devices per bin of their max(|VGS|, |VGD|) [V], peak and held >= 100 ns\\n')
    rows, bcsv = [], []
    for k in ('3.3', '3.3_dc', '3.3_tran', '3.6', '3.6_dc', '3.6_tran'):
        for spec in (True, False):
            for held in (False, True):
                y = np.array([gx(k, r, held) for r in topo if r['spec_L'] == spec])
                cnt = np.histogram(y, bins=edges)[0].tolist()
                rows.append([CLASS_TXT[k], 'L >= spec LG' if spec else 'L = 0.45 um', 'held' if held else 'peak']
                            + cnt)
                bcsv.append([k, int(spec), int(held)] + cnt)
    lines.append(md_table(['runs', 'gate-length class', 'value'] + binlab, rows))
    lines.append('\\nBins are half-open (lower, upper]; values within 1 mV above a bin edge come from numerical '
                 'noise as well as from real overshoot.')
    with open(OUT / 'csv' / 'gate_stress_bins.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(['runs', 'meets_spec_LG', 'held_100ns'] + [f'bin_{a}_{b}V' for a, b in zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:])])
        w.writerows(bcsv)

    # --- devices meeting the spec LG with |VGS| or |VGD| > 3.3 V: list them"""
assert a in s; s=s.replace(a,b)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

```bash
        oth = '3.3' if sup == '3.6' else '3.6'

        lines.append(md_table(['#', 'device', 'block', 'type', 'W/L [um]', 'L meets spec LG', f'{lab} [V]',
                               'where', 'held >= 100 ns [V]', 'DC enabled only [V]', '27 C runs only [V]',
                               f'all {oth} V runs [V]'], rows))

'''
```

## Assistant — 2026-11-25 19:22:02 UTC

```
<!--
SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
-->
# Terminal voltages of every MOS device in slot 7 and its custom pads: results

**IHP sg13cmos5l simulation**, ngspice-47, IHP OSDI models (PSP 103, quasi-static unless stated otherwise). The deck is
the slot-level deck: pads, `pad_ref_rb` and `bias_dist_pd` are KLayout-extracted with the diode fix applied. It has
499 MOS devices, all HV. Method, conditions and limits of this study: [README.md](README.md). Every generated table:
[tables.md](tables.md). Runs of 2026-11-25.

In this file:

- *Gate stress* means max(|VGS|, |VGD|). The source is the electrical source: the lower diffusion terminal for NMOS, the
  higher for PMOS.
- *Peak* means the largest value at any simulated point.
- *Held* means the largest value that stays for at least 100 ns. For DC runs, held = peak.
- *Above 3.3 V* means above 3.301 V. This 1 mV tolerance keeps out VGS = supply, which comes out a few µV above 3.3 V at
  some points.
- *L meets LG* means L ≥ 0.6 µm for HV-NMOS and L ≥ 0.5 µm for HV-PMOS. This is the specification's condition. Every
  other device in the slot has L = 0.45 µm.

## 1. Verdict

| question | answer |
|---|---|
| Largest terminal voltages at **3.3 V nominal**, enabled, DC (15 PVT points; pad −0.2 … 3.3 V; three D2S loads) | \|VGS\| **3.300 V**: the 3.3 V logic, gate at one rail and source at the other. \|VGD\| and \|VDS\| **3.500 V**: S2D pad clamp PMOS `x2s.xlay.x_3` (266.4/0.6 µm, L meets LG). This only happens with the pad driven to −0.2 V: gate at `iovdd` through rppd, drain = pad, VGS ≈ 0. No 0.45 µm device goes above 3.300 V. |
| Same at **3.6 V** (15 PVT points) | \|VGS\| **3.600 V**, \|VGD\| = \|VDS\| **3.800 V** (the same clamp, pad −0.2 V). Gate stress above 3.3 V: **154 of 244** devices with L = 0.45 µm and **74 of 255** with L meeting LG. The 74 are 67 in `ls_up_fs` (leaker PMOS 0.3/20, keeper PMOS 0.3/5), 4 in the S2D pad, 3 in `bg_core_pd`. All of them see VGS or VGD = supply. |
| **Transients and disabled state**, 3.3 V | Some nodes float when the slot is switched off: the spare output `isp` of `bias_dist_pd`, the D2S `vinp` / `vinn`, the S2D `vref` and others. The enable and disable edges kick these nodes beyond the rails, and they stay there until the run ends (tens of µs). Held gate stress up to **3.714 V** on `bias_dist_pd` TG NMOS `xs.xd.xlay.x_8.x_7` (1/0.45 µm, `en`, ff −40 °C). **17** devices with L = 0.45 µm are held above 3.3 V, all `tg_hv` / `bias_dist_pd` switches (section 3). Held \|VDS\| up to **3.955 V** (`bg_core_pd` `xms1_7`, 0.5/12.5 µm). |
| Same, 3.6 V | Held gate stress up to **4.082 V** (`s7_ctrl` switch PMOS `xs.xc.xt10.xp`, 1/0.45 µm, gate at `rc_b` = 3.6 V, `vref` at −0.48 V after the disable edge). Held \|VDS\| up to **4.283 V** (`bg_core_pd` `xms1_7`). |
| Does a device with **L below LG (0.45 µm)** see more than 3.3 V? | **At 3.3 V supply, enabled, DC: no** (max 3.300 V). **At 3.3 V in the disabled state after an enable edge: yes**, held up to 3.714 V on 17 devices (switches next to floating nodes). Peaks during edges reach 3.875 V. **At 3.6 V: yes**, by design: every 0.45 µm logic and switch device sees VGS = 3.6 V (154 in DC). Held up to 4.082 V. The specification states no maximum for L = 0.45 µm. |
| Does a device with L meeting LG see more than 3.3 V? | At 3.3 V, DC, enabled: one device, the S2D clamp PMOS, at 3.500 V gate–drain with the pad at −0.2 V. Its VGS is 0, so it carries no channel current (no HCI). At 3.3 V, held in transients: 4 devices at 3.40 … 3.50 V (that clamp, and D2S pad devices during the `pu late` start-up). At **3.6 V: 74 devices in DC, at VGS = 3.600 V**. That is 0.3 V above the specification's 3.3 V maximum at 27 °C, for the whole 3.6 V corner. |
| \|VDS\| against BVDSS (spec min 5.3 V for HV-NMOS / HV-PMOS) | Held \|VDS\| ≤ 3.955 V (3.3 V) and ≤ 4.283 V (3.6 V): margin ≥ 1.0 V. DC ≤ 3.800 V. Peak \|VDS\| above 5.3 V appears only on internal nodes of the two long-channel 
… [9622 more characters cut]
```

## Assistant — 2026-11-25 19:22:37 UTC

**Tool call: `Bash`**

_Check Markdown table column counts_

```bash
cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/voltage_stress && python3 - <<'EOF'
import re
for f in ['README.md','results.md','tables.md']:
    lines=open(f).read().splitlines()
    i=0; bad=0
    while i<len(lines):
        if lines[i].startswith('|') and i+1<len(lines) and re.match(r'^\|(-+\|)+$',lines[i+1].replace(' ','').replace(':','')):
            def ncol(l):
                l2=re.sub(r'\\\|','',l)
                # inline code may contain unescaped |? count
                return l2.count('|')-1
            n=ncol(lines[i]); j=i+1
            while j<len(lines) and lines[j].startswith('|'):
                if ncol(lines[j])!=n:
                    bad+=1; print(f,j+1,ncol(lines[j]),n,lines[j][:100])
                j+=1
            i=j
        else: i+=1
    print(f,'bad rows',bad)
EOF
```
